> 'Дополнительные функции к перекодировщику
>
> Private Function glKTU(sim, SIM2)
>
> '
>
> ' sogl Макрос
>
> ' Макрос записан 20.07.2013 Тихвинский В.И.
>
> '
>
> glKTU = sim = "а" Or sim = ChrW(1110) Or sim = "у" Or sim = ChrW(1108) Or sim = ChrW(1121) _
>
>  Or sim = ChrW(1167) Or sim = "л" And SIM2 = ChrW(1167)
>
> End Function
>
> Private Function sogl(sim)
>
> '
>
> ' sogl Макрос
>
> ' Макрос записан 20.07.2013 Тихвинский В.И.
>
> '
>
> sogl = (sim = "k" Or sim = "к" Or sim = "g" Or sim = "г" Or sim = "c" Or sim = "ч" Or sim = "н" Or sim = "n" _
>
>  Or sim = "j" Or sim = "Ж" Or sim = "T" Or sim = "Т" Or sim = "D" Or sim = "Д" Or sim = "t" Or sim = "т" _
>
>  Or sim = "d" Or sim = "д" Or sim = "p" Or sim = "п" Or sim = "b" Or sim = "б" Or sim = ChrW(7789) Or sim = ChrW(7693) _
>
>  Or sim = "G" Or sim = "Г" Or sim = ChrW(7749) Or sim = "Ь" Or sim = "~" Or sim = ChrW(241) Or sim = "N" Or sim = "Н" Or sim = ChrW(7751) _
>
>  Or sim = "m" Or sim = "м" Or sim = "y" Or sim = "й" Or sim = "r" Or sim = "р" Or sim = "l" Or sim = "л" Or sim = "L" Or sim = "Л" Or sim = ChrW(7735) _
>
>  Or sim = "v" Or sim = "в" Or sim = "C" Or sim = "ш" Or sim = ChrW(231) Or sim = "S" Or sim = "Ш" Or sim = ChrW(7779) Or sim = "s" Or sim = "с" Or sim = "h" Or sim = "х" _
>
>  Or sim = "J" Or sim = "z" Or sim = "Z") ' J,z,Z дополнительная кодировка для Гарвард-Киото(Harvard-Kyoto)
>
> End Function
>
> Public Function SanRus(s)
>
> '
>
> ' SanRus Макрос
>
> ' Макрос записан 4.08.2013 Тихвинский В.И.
>
> ' Переделан в функцию 17.10.14
>
> '
>
> Dim s1 As String, i As Long
>
> s = s + " "
>
> s1 = ""
>
> For i = 1 To Len(s) - 1
>
>  If (AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 2385 Or AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 2386) And (AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 2305 Or AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 2306 Or AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 2307) Then
>
>   s1 = s1 + Mid(s, i + 1, 1) + Mid(s, i, 1)
>
>   i = i + 1
>
>  Else
>
>   s1 = s1 + Mid(s, i, 1)
>
>  End If
>
> Next i
>
> s = s1
>
> s1 = ""
>
> For i = 1 To Len(s)
>
>   s1 = s1 + Tr(Mid(s, i, 1))
>
> Next i
>
> s1 = s1 + "@"
>
> s = ""
>
> For i = 1 To Len(s1) - 1
>
> 'с придыхательными
>
> If InStr("кгчЖТДтдпб", Mid(s1, i, 1)) <> 0 And Mid(s1, i + 1, 1) = "х" Then
>
>  s = s + Mid(s1, i, 1)
>
> 'гласная а
>
> ElseIf InStr("кгГчЖЬТДНтднпбмйрЛлвшШсх", Mid(s1, i, 1)) <> 0 And InStr("аАиИуУРЪео@", Mid(s1, i + 1, 1)) = 0 Then
>
>  s = s + Mid(s1, i, 1) + "а"
>
> ElseIf Mid(s1, i, 1) <> "@" Then
>
>  s = s + Mid(s1, i, 1)
>
> End If
>
> Next i
>
>  SanRus = s
>
> End Function
>
> Private Function Tr(sim)
>
> '
>
> ' Tr Макрос
>
> ' Макрос записан 03.03.2012 Тихвинский В.И.
>
> '
>
>   'служебные символы
>
>   If sim = " " Then
>
>   Tr = Chr(32)
>
>   ElseIf sim = ChrW(13) Then
>
>   Tr = Chr(13)
>
>   'аваграха
>
>   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2365) Then
>
>    Tr = "'"
>
>   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2404) Then
>
>   Tr = "|"
>
>   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2405) Then
>
>   Tr = "||"
>
>   'слоги
>
>   'гласные и полугласные в роли гласных
>
>   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2309) Then
>
>     Tr = "а"
>
>   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2310) Or sim = ChrW(2366) Then
>
>     Tr = "А"
>
>   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2311) Or sim = ChrW(2367) Then
>
>    Tr = "и"
>
>   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2312) Or sim = ChrW(2368) Then
>
>    Tr = "И"
>
>   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2313) Or sim = ChrW(2369) Then
>
>    Tr = "у"
>
>   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2314) Or sim = ChrW(2370) Then
>
>    Tr = "У"
>
>   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2315) Or sim = ChrW(2371) Then
>
>    Tr = "Р"
>
>   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2400) Or sim = ChrW(2372) Then
>
>    Tr = "Ъ"
>
>  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2319) Or sim = ChrW(2375) Then
>
>     Tr = "е"
>
>  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2320) Or sim = ChrW(2376) Then
>
>     Tr = "аи"
>
>  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2323) Or sim = ChrW(2379) Then
>
>     Tr = "о"
>
>  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2324) Or sim = ChrW(2380) Then
>
>     Tr = "ау"
>
>  'лР
>
>  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2402) Then
>
>     Tr = "@лР"
>
>  'лР
>
>  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2316) Then
>
>     Tr = "лР"
>
>  'лЪ
>
>  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2403) Then
>
>     Tr = "@лЪ"
>
>  'лЪ
>
>  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2401) Then
>
>     Tr = "лЪ"
>
>  'Анунасика
>
>  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2305) Then
>
>     Tr = "№"
>
>  'Анусвара
>
>  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2306) Then
>
>     Tr = "М"
>
>  'оМ дебильный
>
>  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2384) Then
>
>     Tr = "оМ"
>
>  'Вирама
>
>  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2307) Then
>
>    Tr = "Х"
>
>  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2325) Then
>
>    Tr = "к"
>
>  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2326) Then
>
>    Tr = "кх"
>
>  'Аваграха
>
>  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2381) Then
>
>    Tr = "@"
>
>  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2327) Then
>
>    Tr = "г"
>
>  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2328) Then
>
>    Tr = "гх"
>
>  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2329) Then
>
>    Tr = "Г"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2330) Then
>
>     Tr = "ч"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2331) Then
>
>     Tr = "чх"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2332) Then
>

>

>
>     Tr = "Жх"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2334) Then
>
>     Tr = "Ь"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2335) Then

> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2336) Then

>     Tr = "Тх"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2337) Then
>
>     Tr = "Д"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2338) Then
>
>     Tr = "Дх"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2339) Then
>
>     Tr = "Н"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2340) Then
>
>     Tr = "т"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2341) Then
>
>     Tr = "тх"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2342) Then
>
>     Tr = "д"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2343) Then
>
>     Tr = "дх"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2344) Then
>
>     Tr = "н"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2346) Then
>
>     Tr = "п"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2347) Then
>
>     Tr = "пх"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2348) Then
>
>     Tr = "б"

> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2349) Then
>
>     Tr = "бх"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2350) Then
>
>     Tr = "м"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2351) Then
>
>     Tr = "й"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2352) Then
>
>     Tr = "р"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2355) Then
>
>     Tr = "Л"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2354) Then
>
>     Tr = "л"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2357) Then
>
>     Tr = "в"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2358) Then
>
>     Tr = "ш"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2359) Then
>
>     Tr = "Ш"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2360) Then
>
>     Tr = "с"
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2361) Then
>
>     Tr = "х"
>
>  ' _ подчеркивание
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2386) Then
>
>     Tr = "_"
>
> ' * надстрочный штрих
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2385) Then
>
>  Tr = "*"
>
> ' '
>
> ElseIf sim = ChrW(2365) Then
>
>  Tr = "'"
>
> ' цифры

> ElseIf sim >= ChrW(2406) And sim <= ChrW(2415) Then
>
>  Tr = Chr(AscW(sim) - 2358)
>
> Else
>
>  Tr = sim
>
> End If
>
> End Function
>
> Потом закройте редактор VBA(Меню: File-Close)
>
> На переключатель на панели элементов в виде Угольника с карандашом, что-бы выйти из режима конструктора и программа будет работать.

## 11. Тихвинский Виталий Игоревич — 2015-01-17 22:55:50 · 📎 1

17.01.2015, 21:34, "Тихвинский Виталий Игоревич" <tvitaly1@…>:

 В PDF файле посмотрите таблицу транслитераций.

 17.01.2015, 21:27, "Тихвинский Виталий Игоревич" <tvitaly1@…>:
  Рад что заработало.

  Объект не “надпись” должен быть, “поле”. Меню: вид-панели-элементы управления Выбираем поле, и раздвигаем его как в Pain прямоугольник на листе, затем два раза кликните по полю, вы попадете в модуль листа, это все удалите:

  Private Sub TextBox1_Change()

  End Sub

  А это вставьте.

  Private Sub TextBox1_Change()

    s = RusLat_San(TextBox1.Text)

   Cells(1, 1) = s

   TextBox1.Text = s

    Selection.AutoFilter Field:=1, Criteria1:="=" + s + "*", Operator:=xlAnd

  End Sub

  Private Sub TextBox1_KeyPress(ByVal KeyAscii As MSForms.ReturnInteger)

  'если R

  If SanRus(Mid(" " + TextBox1.Text, Len(TextBox1.Text) + 1)) = "Р" Then

   If InStr("РR", ChrW(KeyAscii)) <> 0 Then

     TextBox1.Text = RusLat_San(SanRus(TextBox1.Text) + ChrW(KeyAscii))

     KeyAscii = 0

   End If

  'если Вирама

  ElseIf Mid(" " + TextBox1.Text, Len(TextBox1.Text) + 1) = ChrW(2381) Then

   'если гласная

   If InStr("аАиИуУеЕоОРЪaAiIuUeEoORхh}", ChrW(KeyAscii)) <> 0 Then

     TextBox1.Text = RusLat_San(SanRus(TextBox1.Text) + ChrW(KeyAscii))

     KeyAscii = 0

   End If

  End If

  End Sub

  Дольше вставьте модуль макросов(Меню: вставка-модуль Insert-Module)

  И вставьте в модуль этот код:

  Function RusLat_San(srt)

  '

  ' RusLat_san Макрос

  ' Макрос записан 20.07.2013 Тихвинский В.И.

  ' Переделан в функцию 14.10.14

  '

   Dim i As Long

    s = "@" + srt + "  "

    dev = ""

   s1 = ""

   s = s + " "

   For i = 1 To Len(s) - 3

   'музыкальные знаки и анусвара или анунасика или висарга

    If (Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(784) Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(775) Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ":" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "Щ" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "M" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "М" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "№" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "#" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "Х" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "H" _

    Or AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 7745 Or AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 7747 Or AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 328 Or AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 7717) And (Mid(s, i, 1) = "*" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "_" Or AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 1648 Or AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 817) Then

     s1 = s1 + Mid(s, i + 1, 1) + Mid(s, i, 1)

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "n" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "g" Then

     s1 = s1 + "G"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "р" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "ы" Then

     s1 = s1 + "Р"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "л" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "ы" Then

     s1 = s1 + "лР"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "р" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "Ы" Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ъ"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "л" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "Ы" Then

     s1 = s1 + "лЪ"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "н" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "ь" Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ь"

     i = i + 1

    'Е -кириллица

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "Е" Then

     s1 = s1 + "е"

    'О -кириллица

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "О" Then

     s1 = s1 + "о"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "Й" Then

     s1 = s1 + "аи"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "В" Then

     s1 = s1 + "ау"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "К" Then

     s1 = s1 + "Г"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "З" Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ж"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "П" Then

     s1 = s1 + "Л"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "Ф" Then

     s1 = s1 + "х"

   'устранение КТ+lat придыхательные

    ElseIf InStr("кгчЖТДтдпб", Mid(s, i, 1)) <> 0 And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "h" Then

     s1 = s1 + Mid(s, i, 1) + "х"

     i = i + 1

    'устранение кодировки КТЮ

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "а" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "а" Then

     s1 = s1 + "А"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1110) And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(1110) Then

     s1 = s1 + "И"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1110) Then

     s1 = s1 + "и"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "у" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "у" Then

     s1 = s1 + "У"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "а" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(1111) And Not glKTU(Mid(s, i + 2, 1), Mid(s, i + 3, 1)) Then

     s1 = s1 + "аи"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "а" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "в" And Not glKTU(Mid(s, i + 2, 1), Mid(s, i + 3, 1)) Then

     s1 = s1 + "ау"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1167) And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(1167) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ъ"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1167) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Р"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1111) Then

     s1 = s1 + "й"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1108) Then

     s1 = s1 + "е"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1121) Then

     s1 = s1 + "о"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1189) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Г"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1115) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ж"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1114) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ь"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(708) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Л"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "т" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(61476) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Т"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "д" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(61476) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Д"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "н" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(61476) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Н"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "ш" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(61476) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ш"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "Р" And (Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "Р" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "R") Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ъ"

     i = i + 1

      'запретные символы, мягкий знак и надстрочная дуга

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) <> "ь" And Mid(s, i, 1) <> ChrW(865) Then

       s1 = s1 + Mid(s, i, 1)

    End If

   Next i

   s = s1 + "  "

   For i = 2 To Len(s) - 2

    sim0 = Mid(s, i - 1, 1)

   If sogl(sim0) And (Mid(s, i, 1) = "a" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "а") Then

    If Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "i" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "и" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "u" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "у" Then

     sim = Mid(s, i, 1) + Mid(s, i + 1, 1) + "_"

     i = i + 1

    Else

     sim = ""

    End If

   ElseIf sogl(sim0) And (Mid(s, i, 1) = "А" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "A" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(257) Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "и" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "i" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "И" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "I" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(299) _

   Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "у" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "u" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "У" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "U" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(363) _

   Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "Р" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "R" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "Ъ" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "}" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(7771) Or Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(7773) _

   Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "о" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "o" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "е" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "e") Then

     sim = Mid(s, i, 1) + "_"

     If Mid(s, i, 1) = "R" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "R" Then

      sim = "}_"

      i = i + 1

     End If

   Else

    sim0 = Mid(s, i - 1, 1)

    sim = Mid(s, i, 1)

    If Mid(s, i, 1) = "R" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "R" Then

     sim = "}"

     i = i + 1

    End If

    sim1 = Mid(s, i + 1, 1)

   'придыхательные и ||

   If ((sim1 = "h" Or sim1 = "х") And (sim = "k" Or sim = "к" Or sim = "g" Or sim = "г" Or sim = "c" Or sim = "ч" _

   Or sim = "j" Or sim = "Ж" Or sim = "T" Or sim = "Т" Or sim = "D" Or sim = "Д" Or sim = "t" Or sim = "т" _

   Or sim = "d" Or sim = "д" Or sim = "p" Or sim = "п" Or sim = "b" Or sim = "б" Or sim = ChrW(7789) Or sim = ChrW(7693))) Or sim = "|" And sim1 = "|" Or sim = "n" And sim1 = "g" _

   Or sim = "a" And sim1 = "i" Or sim = "а" And sim1 = "и" Or sim = "a" And sim1 = "u" Or sim = "а" And sim1 = "у" Then

    sim = sim + sim1

    i = i + 1

   End If

   End If

   'служебные символы

    If sim = " " Then

    dev = dev + Chr(32)

    ElseIf sim = Chr(13) Then

    dev = dev + Chr(13)

    'табуляция

    ElseIf sim = Chr(10) Then

     dev = dev + Chr(10)

    'табуляция

    ElseIf sim = Chr(9) Then

     dev = dev + Chr(9)

     'Аваграха

    ElseIf sim = "'" Or sim = ChrW(8216) Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2365)

    ElseIf sim = "|" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2404)

    ElseIf sim = "||" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2405)

    'слоги

    'гласные и полугласные в роли гласных не в составе слога

    ElseIf sim = "а" Or sim = "a" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2309)

    ElseIf sim = "А" Or sim = "A" Or sim = ChrW(257) Then

        dev = dev + ChrW(2310)

    ElseIf sim = "и" Or sim = "i" Then

       dev = dev + ChrW(2311)

    ElseIf sim = "И" Or sim = "I" Or sim = ChrW(299) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2312)

    ElseIf sim = "у" Or sim = "u" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2313)

    ElseIf sim = "У" Or sim = "U" Or sim = ChrW(363) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2314)

    ElseIf sim = "Р" Or sim = "R" Or sim = ChrW(7771) Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2315)

    ElseIf sim = "Ъ" Or sim = "}" Or sim = ChrW(7773) Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2400)

    ElseIf sim = "А_" Or sim = "A_" Or sim = ChrW(257) + "_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2366)

    ElseIf sim = "и_" Or sim = "i_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2367)

    ElseIf sim = "И_" Or sim = "I_" Or sim = ChrW(299) + "_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2368)

    ElseIf sim = "у_" Or sim = "u_" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2369)

    ElseIf sim = "У_" Or sim = "U_" Or sim = ChrW(363) + "_" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2370)

    ElseIf sim = "Р_" Or sim = "R_" Or sim = ChrW(7771) + "_" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2371)

    ElseIf sim = "Ъ_" Or sim = "}_" Or sim = ChrW(7773) + "_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2372)

    'дифтонги

    ElseIf sim = "е" Or sim = "e" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2319)

    ElseIf sim = "аи" Or sim = "ai" Then

       dev = dev + ChrW(2320)

    ElseIf sim = "о" Or sim = "o" Then

       dev = dev + ChrW(2323)

    ElseIf sim = "ау" Or sim = "au" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2324)

    ElseIf sim = "е_" Or sim = "e_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2375)

    ElseIf sim = "аи_" Or sim = "ai_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2376)

    ElseIf sim = "о_" Or sim = "o_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2379)

    ElseIf sim = "ау_" Or sim = "au_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2380)

    'Анунасика

    ElseIf sim = "№" Or sim = "#" Or sim = ChrW(328) Or sim = ChrW(784) Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2305)

    'Анусвара 7747 - c нижней точкой, 775 - верхняя точка

    ElseIf sim = "М" Or sim = "M" Or sim = ChrW(775) Or sim = ChrW(7745) Or sim = ChrW(7747) Or sim = "Щ" Then

        dev = dev + ChrW(2306)

    'Висарга

    ElseIf sim = "Х" Or sim = "H" Or sim = ChrW(7717) Or sim = "%" Or sim = ":" Then

        dev = dev + ChrW(2307)

    'согласные

    ElseIf sim = "к" Or sim = "k" Then

       dev = dev + ChrW(2325)

    ElseIf sim = "кх" Or sim = "kh" Then

    dev = dev + ChrW(2326)

    ElseIf sim = "г" Or sim = "g" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2327)

    ElseIf sim = "гх" Or sim = "gh" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2328)

    ElseIf sim = "Г" Or sim = "G" Or sim = ChrW(7749) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2329)

    ElseIf sim = "ч" Or sim = "c" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2330)

    ElseIf sim = "чх" Or sim = "ch" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2331)

    ElseIf sim = "Ж" Or sim = "j" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2332)

    ElseIf sim = "Жх" Or sim = "jh" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2333)

    ElseIf sim = "Ь" Or sim = "~" Or sim = "J" Or sim = ChrW(241) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2334)

    ElseIf sim = "Т" Or sim = "T" Or sim = ChrW(7789) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2335)

    ElseIf sim = "Тх" Or sim = "Th" Or sim = ChrW(7789) + "h" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2336)

    ElseIf sim = "Д" Or sim = "D" Or sim = ChrW(7693) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2337)

    ElseIf sim = "Дх" Or sim = "Dh" Or sim = ChrW(7693) + "h" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2338)

    ElseIf sim = "Н" Or sim = "N" Or sim = ChrW(7751) Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2339)

    ElseIf sim = "т" Or sim = "t" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2340)

    ElseIf sim = "тх" Or sim = "th" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2341)

    ElseIf sim = "д" Or sim = "d" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2342)

   ElseIf sim = "дх" Or sim = "dh" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2343)

   ElseIf sim = "н" Or sim = "n" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2344)

   ElseIf sim = "п" Or sim = "p" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2346)

   ElseIf sim = "пх" Or sim = "ph" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2347)

   ElseIf sim = "б" Or sim = "b" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2348)

   ElseIf sim = "бх" Or sim = "bh" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2349)

   ElseIf sim = "м" Or sim = "m" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2350)

   ElseIf sim = "й" Or sim = "y" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2351)

   ElseIf sim = "р" Or sim = "r" Then

        dev = dev + ChrW(2352)

   ElseIf sim = "Л" Or sim = "L" Or sim = ChrW(7735) Then

       dev = dev + ChrW(2355)

   ElseIf sim = "л" Or sim = "l" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2354)

   ElseIf sim = "в" Or sim = "v" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2357)

   ElseIf sim = "ш" Or sim = "C" Or sim = "z" Or sim = "Z" Or sim = ChrW(231) Or sim = ChrW(347) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2358)

   ElseIf sim = "Ш" Or sim = "S" Or sim = ChrW(7779) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2359)

   ElseIf sim = "с" Or sim = "s" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2360)

   ElseIf sim = "х" Or sim = "h" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2361)

  ' цифры

  ElseIf sim >= "0" And sim <= "9" Then

   dev = dev + ChrW(Asc(sim) + 2358)

  ' _ подчеркивание

  ElseIf sim = "_" Or sim = ChrW(817) Then

   dev = dev + ChrW(2386)

  ' * надстрочный штрих

  ElseIf sim = "*" Or sim = ChrW(1648) Then

   dev = dev + ChrW(2385)

  ' '

  ElseIf sim = "'" Or sim = ChrW(8216) Then

   dev = dev + ChrW(2365)

  Else

   dev = dev + sim

  End If

  If Len(sim) = 2 Then

   'избавляемся от придыхания

   If Mid(sim, 2) = "х" Or Mid(sim, 2) = "h" Then

    sim = Mid(sim, 1, 1)

    sim1 = Mid(s, i + 1, 1)

   End If

  End If

  If sogl(sim) And (sogl(sim1) Or sim1 = " " Or sim1 = "|" Or sim1 = ChrW(13) Or sim1 = ChrW(10) Or sim1 = ChrW(9) Or sim1 = "," Or sim1 = ".") Then

    'вирама

    dev = dev + ChrW(2381)

  End If

  Next i

   RusLat_San = dev

  End Function

  'Дополнительные функции к перекодировщику

  Private Function glKTU(sim, SIM2)

  '

  ' sogl Макрос

  ' Макрос записан 20.07.2013 Тихвинский В.И.

  '

  glKTU = sim = "а" Or sim = ChrW(1110) Or sim = "у" Or sim = ChrW(1108) Or sim = ChrW(1121) _

   Or sim = ChrW(1167) Or sim = "л" And SIM2 = ChrW(1167)

  End Function

  Private Function sogl(sim)

  '

  ' sogl Макрос

  ' Макрос записан 20.07.2013 Тихвинский В.И.

  '

  sogl = (sim = "k" Or sim = "к" Or sim = "g" Or sim = "г" Or sim = "c" Or sim = "ч" Or sim = "н" Or sim = "n" _

   Or sim = "j" Or sim = "Ж" Or sim = "T" Or sim = "Т" Or sim = "D" Or sim = "Д" Or sim = "t" Or sim = "т" _

   Or sim = "d" Or sim = "д" Or sim = "p" Or sim = "п" Or sim = "b" Or sim = "б" Or sim = ChrW(7789) Or sim = ChrW(7693) _

   Or sim = "G" Or sim = "Г" Or sim = ChrW(7749) Or sim = "Ь" Or sim = "~" Or sim = ChrW(241) Or sim = "N" Or sim = "Н" Or sim = ChrW(7751) _

   Or sim = "m" Or sim = "м" Or sim = "y" Or sim = "й" Or sim = "r" Or sim = "р" Or sim = "l" Or sim = "л" Or sim = "L" Or sim = "Л" Or sim = ChrW(7735) _

   Or sim = "v" Or sim = "в" Or sim = "C" Or sim = "ш" Or sim = ChrW(231) Or sim = "S" Or sim = "Ш" Or sim = ChrW(7779) Or sim = "s" Or sim = "с" Or sim = "h" Or sim = "х" _

   Or sim = "J" Or sim = "z" Or sim = "Z") ' J,z,Z дополнительная кодировка для Гарвард-Киото(Harvard-Kyoto)

  End Function

  Public Function SanRus(s)

  '

  ' SanRus Макрос

  ' Макрос записан 4.08.2013 Тихвинский В.И.

  ' Переделан в функцию 17.10.14

  '

  Dim s1 As String, i As Long

  s = s + " "

  s1 = ""

  For i = 1 To Len(s) - 1

   If (AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 2385 Or AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 2386) And (AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 2305 Or AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 2306 Or AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 2307) Then

    s1 = s1 + Mid(s, i + 1, 1) + Mid(s, i, 1)

    i = i + 1

   Else

    s1 = s1 + Mid(s, i, 1)

   End If

  Next i

  s = s1

  s1 = ""

  For i = 1 To Len(s)

    s1 = s1 + Tr(Mid(s, i, 1))

  Next i

  s1 = s1 + "@"

  s = ""

  For i = 1 To Len(s1) - 1

  'с придыхательными

  If InStr("кгчЖТДтдпб", Mid(s1, i, 1)) <> 0 And Mid(s1, i + 1, 1) = "х" Then

   s = s + Mid(s1, i, 1)

  'гласная а

  ElseIf InStr("кгГчЖЬТДНтднпбмйрЛлвшШсх", Mid(s1, i, 1)) <> 0 And InStr("аАиИуУРЪео@", Mid(s1, i + 1, 1)) = 0 Then

   s = s + Mid(s1, i, 1) + "а"

  ElseIf Mid(s1, i, 1) <> "@" Then

   s = s + Mid(s1, i, 1)

  End If

  Next i

   SanRus = s

  End Function

  Private Function Tr(sim)

  '

  ' Tr Макрос

  ' Макрос записан 03.03.2012 Тихвинский В.И.

  '

    'служебные символы

    If sim = " " Then

    Tr = Chr(32)

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(13) Then

    Tr = Chr(13)

    'аваграха

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2365) Then

     Tr = "'"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2404) Then

    Tr = "|"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2405) Then

    Tr = "||"

    'слоги

    'гласные и полугласные в роли гласных

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2309) Then

      Tr = "а"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2310) Or sim = ChrW(2366) Then

      Tr = "А"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2311) Or sim = ChrW(2367) Then

     Tr = "и"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2312) Or sim = ChrW(2368) Then

     Tr = "И"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2313) Or sim = ChrW(2369) Then

     Tr = "у"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2314) Or sim = ChrW(2370) Then

     Tr = "У"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2315) Or sim = ChrW(2371) Then

     Tr = "Р"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2400) Or sim = ChrW(2372) Then

     Tr = "Ъ"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2319) Or sim = ChrW(2375) Then

      Tr = "е"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2320) Or sim = ChrW(2376) Then

      Tr = "аи"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2323) Or sim = ChrW(2379) Then

      Tr = "о"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2324) Or sim = ChrW(2380) Then

      Tr = "ау"

   'лР

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2402) Then

      Tr = "@лР"

   'лР

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2316) Then

      Tr = "лР"

   'лЪ

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2403) Then

      Tr = "@лЪ"

   'лЪ

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2401) Then

      Tr = "лЪ"

   'Анунасика

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2305) Then

      Tr = "№"

   'Анусвара

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2306) Then

      Tr = "М"

   'оМ дебильный

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2384) Then

      Tr = "оМ"

   'Вирама

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2307) Then

     Tr = "Х"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2325) Then

     Tr = "к"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2326) Then

     Tr = "кх"

   'Аваграха

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2381) Then

     Tr = "@"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2327) Then

     Tr = "г"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2328) Then

     Tr = "гх"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2329) Then

     Tr = "Г"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2330) Then

      Tr = "ч"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2331) Then

      Tr = "чх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2332) Then

      Tr = "Ж"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2333) Then

      Tr = "Жх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2334) Then

      Tr = "Ь"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2335) Then

      Tr = "Т"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2336) Then

      Tr = "Тх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2337) Then

      Tr = "Д"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2338) Then

      Tr = "Дх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2339) Then

      Tr = "Н"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2340) Then

      Tr = "т"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2341) Then

      Tr = "тх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2342) Then

      Tr = "д"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2343) Then

      Tr = "дх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2344) Then

      Tr = "н"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2346) Then

      Tr = "п"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2347) Then

      Tr = "пх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2348) Then

      Tr = "б"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2349) Then

      Tr = "бх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2350) Then

      Tr = "м"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2351) Then

      Tr = "й"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2352) Then

      Tr = "р"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2355) Then

      Tr = "Л"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2354) Then

      Tr = "л"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2357) Then

      Tr = "в"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2358) Then

      Tr = "ш"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2359) Then

      Tr = "Ш"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2360) Then

      Tr = "с"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2361) Then

      Tr = "х"

   ' _ подчеркивание

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2386) Then

      Tr = "_"

  ' * надстрочный штрих

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2385) Then

   Tr = "*"

  ' '

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2365) Then

   Tr = "'"

  ' цифры

  ElseIf sim >= ChrW(2406) And sim <= ChrW(2415) Then

   Tr = Chr(AscW(sim) - 2358)

  Else

   Tr = sim

  End If

  End Function

  Потом закройте редактор VBA(Меню: File-Close)

  На переключатель на панели элементов в виде Угольника с карандашом, что-бы выйти из режима конструктора и программа будет работать.Рад что заработало.

  Объект не “надпись” должен быть, “поле”. Меню: вид-панели-элементы управления Выбираем поле, и раздвигаем его как в Pain прямоугольник на листе, затем два раза кликните по полю, вы попадете в модуль листа, это все удалите:

  Private Sub TextBox1_Change()

  End Sub

  А это вставьте.

  Private Sub TextBox1_Change()

    s = RusLat_San(TextBox1.Text)

   Cells(1, 1) = s

   TextBox1.Text = s

    Selection.AutoFilter Field:=1, Criteria1:="=" + s + "*", Operator:=xlAnd

  End Sub

  Private Sub TextBox1_KeyPress(ByVal KeyAscii As MSForms.ReturnInteger)

  'если R

  If SanRus(Mid(" " + TextBox1.Text, Len(TextBox1.Text) + 1)) = "Р" Then

   If InStr("РR", ChrW(KeyAscii)) <> 0 Then

     TextBox1.Text = RusLat_San(SanRus(TextBox1.Text) + ChrW(KeyAscii))

     KeyAscii = 0

   End If

  'если Вирама

  ElseIf Mid(" " + TextBox1.Text, Len(TextBox1.Text) + 1) = ChrW(2381) Then

   'если гласная

   If InStr("аАиИуУеЕоОРЪaAiIuUeEoORхh}", ChrW(KeyAscii)) <> 0 Then

     TextBox1.Text = RusLat_San(SanRus(TextBox1.Text) + ChrW(KeyAscii))

     KeyAscii = 0

   End If

  End If

  End Sub

  Дольше вставьте модуль макросов(Меню: вставка-модуль Insert-Module)

  И вставьте в модуль этот код:

  Function RusLat_San(srt)

  '

  ' RusLat_san Макрос

  ' Макрос записан 20.07.2013 Тихвинский В.И.

  ' Переделан в функцию 14.10.14

  '

   Dim i As Long

    s = "@" + srt + "  "

    dev = ""

   s1 = ""

   s = s + " "

   For i = 1 To Len(s) - 3

   'музыкальные знаки и анусвара или анунасика или висарга

    If (Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(784) Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(775) Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ":" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "Щ" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "M" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "М" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "№" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "#" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "Х" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "H" _

    Or AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 7745 Or AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 7747 Or AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 328 Or AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 7717) And (Mid(s, i, 1) = "*" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "_" Or AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 1648 Or AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 817) Then

     s1 = s1 + Mid(s, i + 1, 1) + Mid(s, i, 1)

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "n" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "g" Then

     s1 = s1 + "G"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "р" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "ы" Then

     s1 = s1 + "Р"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "л" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "ы" Then

     s1 = s1 + "лР"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "р" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "Ы" Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ъ"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "л" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "Ы" Then

     s1 = s1 + "лЪ"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "н" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "ь" Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ь"

     i = i + 1

    'Е -кириллица

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "Е" Then

     s1 = s1 + "е"

    'О -кириллица

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "О" Then

     s1 = s1 + "о"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "Й" Then

     s1 = s1 + "аи"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "В" Then

     s1 = s1 + "ау"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "К" Then

     s1 = s1 + "Г"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "З" Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ж"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "П" Then

     s1 = s1 + "Л"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "Ф" Then

     s1 = s1 + "х"

   'устранение КТ+lat придыхательные

    ElseIf InStr("кгчЖТДтдпб", Mid(s, i, 1)) <> 0 And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "h" Then

     s1 = s1 + Mid(s, i, 1) + "х"

     i = i + 1

    'устранение кодировки КТЮ

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "а" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "а" Then

     s1 = s1 + "А"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1110) And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(1110) Then

     s1 = s1 + "И"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1110) Then

     s1 = s1 + "и"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "у" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "у" Then

     s1 = s1 + "У"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "а" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(1111) And Not glKTU(Mid(s, i + 2, 1), Mid(s, i + 3, 1)) Then

     s1 = s1 + "аи"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "а" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "в" And Not glKTU(Mid(s, i + 2, 1), Mid(s, i + 3, 1)) Then

     s1 = s1 + "ау"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1167) And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(1167) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ъ"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1167) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Р"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1111) Then

     s1 = s1 + "й"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1108) Then

     s1 = s1 + "е"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1121) Then

     s1 = s1 + "о"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1189) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Г"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1115) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ж"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1114) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ь"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(708) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Л"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "т" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(61476) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Т"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "д" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(61476) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Д"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "н" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(61476) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Н"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "ш" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(61476) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ш"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "Р" And (Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "Р" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "R") Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ъ"

     i = i + 1

      'запретные символы, мягкий знак и надстрочная дуга

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) <> "ь" And Mid(s, i, 1) <> ChrW(865) Then

       s1 = s1 + Mid(s, i, 1)

    End If

   Next i

   s = s1 + "  "

   For i = 2 To Len(s) - 2

    sim0 = Mid(s, i - 1, 1)

   If sogl(sim0) And (Mid(s, i, 1) = "a" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "а") Then

    If Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "i" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "и" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "u" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "у" Then

     sim = Mid(s, i, 1) + Mid(s, i + 1, 1) + "_"

     i = i + 1

    Else

     sim = ""

    End If

   ElseIf sogl(sim0) And (Mid(s, i, 1) = "А" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "A" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(257) Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "и" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "i" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "И" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "I" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(299) _

   Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "у" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "u" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "У" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "U" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(363) _

   Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "Р" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "R" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "Ъ" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "}" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(7771) Or Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(7773) _

   Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "о" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "o" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "е" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "e") Then

     sim = Mid(s, i, 1) + "_"

     If Mid(s, i, 1) = "R" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "R" Then

      sim = "}_"

      i = i + 1

     End If

   Else

    sim0 = Mid(s, i - 1, 1)

    sim = Mid(s, i, 1)

    If Mid(s, i, 1) = "R" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "R" Then

     sim = "}"

     i = i + 1

    End If

    sim1 = Mid(s, i + 1, 1)

   'придыхательные и ||

   If ((sim1 = "h" Or sim1 = "х") And (sim = "k" Or sim = "к" Or sim = "g" Or sim = "г" Or sim = "c" Or sim = "ч" _

   Or sim = "j" Or sim = "Ж" Or sim = "T" Or sim = "Т" Or sim = "D" Or sim = "Д" Or sim = "t" Or sim = "т" _

   Or sim = "d" Or sim = "д" Or sim = "p" Or sim = "п" Or sim = "b" Or sim = "б" Or sim = ChrW(7789) Or sim = ChrW(7693))) Or sim = "|" And sim1 = "|" Or sim = "n" And sim1 = "g" _

   Or sim = "a" And sim1 = "i" Or sim = "а" And sim1 = "и" Or sim = "a" And sim1 = "u" Or sim = "а" And sim1 = "у" Then

    sim = sim + sim1

    i = i + 1

   End If

   End If

   'служебные символы

    If sim = " " Then

    dev = dev + Chr(32)

    ElseIf sim = Chr(13) Then

    dev = dev + Chr(13)

    'табуляция

    ElseIf sim = Chr(10) Then

     dev = dev + Chr(10)

    'табуляция

    ElseIf sim = Chr(9) Then

     dev = dev + Chr(9)

     'Аваграха

    ElseIf sim = "'" Or sim = ChrW(8216) Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2365)

    ElseIf sim = "|" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2404)

    ElseIf sim = "||" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2405)

    'слоги

    'гласные и полугласные в роли гласных не в составе слога

    ElseIf sim = "а" Or sim = "a" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2309)

    ElseIf sim = "А" Or sim = "A" Or sim = ChrW(257) Then

        dev = dev + ChrW(2310)

    ElseIf sim = "и" Or sim = "i" Then

       dev = dev + ChrW(2311)

    ElseIf sim = "И" Or sim = "I" Or sim = ChrW(299) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2312)

    ElseIf sim = "у" Or sim = "u" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2313)

    ElseIf sim = "У" Or sim = "U" Or sim = ChrW(363) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2314)

    ElseIf sim = "Р" Or sim = "R" Or sim = ChrW(7771) Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2315)

    ElseIf sim = "Ъ" Or sim = "}" Or sim = ChrW(7773) Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2400)

    ElseIf sim = "А_" Or sim = "A_" Or sim = ChrW(257) + "_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2366)

    ElseIf sim = "и_" Or sim = "i_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2367)

    ElseIf sim = "И_" Or sim = "I_" Or sim = ChrW(299) + "_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2368)

    ElseIf sim = "у_" Or sim = "u_" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2369)

    ElseIf sim = "У_" Or sim = "U_" Or sim = ChrW(363) + "_" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2370)

    ElseIf sim = "Р_" Or sim = "R_" Or sim = ChrW(7771) + "_" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2371)

    ElseIf sim = "Ъ_" Or sim = "}_" Or sim = ChrW(7773) + "_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2372)

    'дифтонги

    ElseIf sim = "е" Or sim = "e" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2319)

    ElseIf sim = "аи" Or sim = "ai" Then

       dev = dev + ChrW(2320)

    ElseIf sim = "о" Or sim = "o" Then

       dev = dev + ChrW(2323)

    ElseIf sim = "ау" Or sim = "au" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2324)

    ElseIf sim = "е_" Or sim = "e_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2375)

    ElseIf sim = "аи_" Or sim = "ai_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2376)

    ElseIf sim = "о_" Or sim = "o_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2379)

    ElseIf sim = "ау_" Or sim = "au_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2380)

    'Анунасика

    ElseIf sim = "№" Or sim = "#" Or sim = ChrW(328) Or sim = ChrW(784) Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2305)

    'Анусвара 7747 - c нижней точкой, 775 - верхняя точка

    ElseIf sim = "М" Or sim = "M" Or sim = ChrW(775) Or sim = ChrW(7745) Or sim = ChrW(7747) Or sim = "Щ" Then

        dev = dev + ChrW(2306)

    'Висарга

    ElseIf sim = "Х" Or sim = "H" Or sim = ChrW(7717) Or sim = "%" Or sim = ":" Then

        dev = dev + ChrW(2307)

    'согласные

    ElseIf sim = "к" Or sim = "k" Then

       dev = dev + ChrW(2325)

    ElseIf sim = "кх" Or sim = "kh" Then

    dev = dev + ChrW(2326)

    ElseIf sim = "г" Or sim = "g" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2327)

    ElseIf sim = "гх" Or sim = "gh" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2328)

    ElseIf sim = "Г" Or sim = "G" Or sim = ChrW(7749) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2329)

    ElseIf sim = "ч" Or sim = "c" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2330)

    ElseIf sim = "чх" Or sim = "ch" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2331)

    ElseIf sim = "Ж" Or sim = "j" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2332)

    ElseIf sim = "Жх" Or sim = "jh" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2333)

    ElseIf sim = "Ь" Or sim = "~" Or sim = "J" Or sim = ChrW(241) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2334)

    ElseIf sim = "Т" Or sim = "T" Or sim = ChrW(7789) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2335)

    ElseIf sim = "Тх" Or sim = "Th" Or sim = ChrW(7789) + "h" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2336)

    ElseIf sim = "Д" Or sim = "D" Or sim = ChrW(7693) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2337)

    ElseIf sim = "Дх" Or sim = "Dh" Or sim = ChrW(7693) + "h" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2338)

    ElseIf sim = "Н" Or sim = "N" Or sim = ChrW(7751) Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2339)

    ElseIf sim = "т" Or sim = "t" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2340)

    ElseIf sim = "тх" Or sim = "th" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2341)

    ElseIf sim = "д" Or sim = "d" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2342)

   ElseIf sim = "дх" Or sim = "dh" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2343)

   ElseIf sim = "н" Or sim = "n" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2344)

   ElseIf sim = "п" Or sim = "p" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2346)

   ElseIf sim = "пх" Or sim = "ph" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2347)

   ElseIf sim = "б" Or sim = "b" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2348)

   ElseIf sim = "бх" Or sim = "bh" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2349)

   ElseIf sim = "м" Or sim = "m" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2350)

   ElseIf sim = "й" Or sim = "y" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2351)

   ElseIf sim = "р" Or sim = "r" Then

        dev = dev + ChrW(2352)

   ElseIf sim = "Л" Or sim = "L" Or sim = ChrW(7735) Then

       dev = dev + ChrW(2355)

   ElseIf sim = "л" Or sim = "l" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2354)

   ElseIf sim = "в" Or sim = "v" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2357)

   ElseIf sim = "ш" Or sim = "C" Or sim = "z" Or sim = "Z" Or sim = ChrW(231) Or sim = ChrW(347) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2358)

   ElseIf sim = "Ш" Or sim = "S" Or sim = ChrW(7779) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2359)

   ElseIf sim = "с" Or sim = "s" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2360)

   ElseIf sim = "х" Or sim = "h" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2361)

  ' цифры

  ElseIf sim >= "0" And sim <= "9" Then

   dev = dev + ChrW(Asc(sim) + 2358)

  ' _ подчеркивание

  ElseIf sim = "_" Or sim = ChrW(817) Then

   dev = dev + ChrW(2386)

  ' * надстрочный штрих

  ElseIf sim = "*" Or sim = ChrW(1648) Then

   dev = dev + ChrW(2385)

  ' '

  ElseIf sim = "'" Or sim = ChrW(8216) Then

   dev = dev + ChrW(2365)

  Else

   dev = dev + sim

  End If

  If Len(sim) = 2 Then

   'избавляемся от придыхания

   If Mid(sim, 2) = "х" Or Mid(sim, 2) = "h" Then

    sim = Mid(sim, 1, 1)

    sim1 = Mid(s, i + 1, 1)

   End If

  End If

  If sogl(sim) And (sogl(sim1) Or sim1 = " " Or sim1 = "|" Or sim1 = ChrW(13) Or sim1 = ChrW(10) Or sim1 = ChrW(9) Or sim1 = "," Or sim1 = ".") Then

    'вирама

    dev = dev + ChrW(2381)

  End If

  Next i

   RusLat_San = dev

  End Function

  'Дополнительные функции к перекодировщику

  Private Function glKTU(sim, SIM2)

  '

  ' sogl Макрос

  ' Макрос записан 20.07.2013 Тихвинский В.И.

  '

  glKTU = sim = "а" Or sim = ChrW(1110) Or sim = "у" Or sim = ChrW(1108) Or sim = ChrW(1121) _

   Or sim = ChrW(1167) Or sim = "л" And SIM2 = ChrW(1167)

  End Function

  Private Function sogl(sim)

  '

  ' sogl Макрос

  ' Макрос записан 20.07.2013 Тихвинский В.И.

  '

  sogl = (sim = "k" Or sim = "к" Or sim = "g" Or sim = "г" Or sim = "c" Or sim = "ч" Or sim = "н" Or sim = "n" _

   Or sim = "j" Or sim = "Ж" Or sim = "T" Or sim = "Т" Or sim = "D" Or sim = "Д" Or sim = "t" Or sim = "т" _

   Or sim = "d" Or sim = "д" Or sim = "p" Or sim = "п" Or sim = "b" Or sim = "б" Or sim = ChrW(7789) Or sim = ChrW(7693) _

   Or sim = "G" Or sim = "Г" Or sim = ChrW(7749) Or sim = "Ь" Or sim = "~" Or sim = ChrW(241) Or sim = "N" Or sim = "Н" Or sim = ChrW(7751) _

   Or sim = "m" Or sim = "м" Or sim = "y" Or sim = "й" Or sim = "r" Or sim = "р" Or sim = "l" Or sim = "л" Or sim = "L" Or sim = "Л" Or sim = ChrW(7735) _

   Or sim = "v" Or sim = "в" Or sim = "C" Or sim = "ш" Or sim = ChrW(231) Or sim = "S" Or sim = "Ш" Or sim = ChrW(7779) Or sim = "s" Or sim = "с" Or sim = "h" Or sim = "х" _

   Or sim = "J" Or sim = "z" Or sim = "Z") ' J,z,Z дополнительная кодировка для Гарвард-Киото(Harvard-Kyoto)

  End Function

  Public Function SanRus(s)

  '

  ' SanRus Макрос

  ' Макрос записан 4.08.2013 Тихвинский В.И.

  ' Переделан в функцию 17.10.14

  '

  Dim s1 As String, i As Long

  s = s + " "

  s1 = ""

  For i = 1 To Len(s) - 1

   If (AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 2385 Or AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 2386) And (AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 2305 Or AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 2306 Or AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 2307) Then

    s1 = s1 + Mid(s, i + 1, 1) + Mid(s, i, 1)

    i = i + 1

   Else

    s1 = s1 + Mid(s, i, 1)

   End If

  Next i

  s = s1

  s1 = ""

  For i = 1 To Len(s)

    s1 = s1 + Tr(Mid(s, i, 1))

  Next i

  s1 = s1 + "@"

  s = ""

  For i = 1 To Len(s1) - 1

  'с придыхательными

  If InStr("кгчЖТДтдпб", Mid(s1, i, 1)) <> 0 And Mid(s1, i + 1, 1) = "х" Then

   s = s + Mid(s1, i, 1)

  'гласная а

  ElseIf InStr("кгГчЖЬТДНтднпбмйрЛлвшШсх", Mid(s1, i, 1)) <> 0 And InStr("аАиИуУРЪео@", Mid(s1, i + 1, 1)) = 0 Then

   s = s + Mid(s1, i, 1) + "а"

  ElseIf Mid(s1, i, 1) <> "@" Then

   s = s + Mid(s1, i, 1)

  End If

  Next i

   SanRus = s

  End Function

  Private Function Tr(sim)

  '

  ' Tr Макрос

  ' Макрос записан 03.03.2012 Тихвинский В.И.

  '

    'служебные символы

    If sim = " " Then

    Tr = Chr(32)

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(13) Then

    Tr = Chr(13)

    'аваграха

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2365) Then

     Tr = "'"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2404) Then

    Tr = "|"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2405) Then

    Tr = "||"

    'слоги

    'гласные и полугласные в роли гласных

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2309) Then

      Tr = "а"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2310) Or sim = ChrW(2366) Then

      Tr = "А"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2311) Or sim = ChrW(2367) Then

     Tr = "и"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2312) Or sim = ChrW(2368) Then

     Tr = "И"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2313) Or sim = ChrW(2369) Then

     Tr = "у"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2314) Or sim = ChrW(2370) Then

     Tr = "У"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2315) Or sim = ChrW(2371) Then

     Tr = "Р"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2400) Or sim = ChrW(2372) Then

     Tr = "Ъ"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2319) Or sim = ChrW(2375) Then

      Tr = "е"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2320) Or sim = ChrW(2376) Then

      Tr = "аи"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2323) Or sim = ChrW(2379) Then

      Tr = "о"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2324) Or sim = ChrW(2380) Then

      Tr = "ау"

   'лР

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2402) Then

      Tr = "@лР"

   'лР

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2316) Then

      Tr = "лР"

   'лЪ

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2403) Then

      Tr = "@лЪ"

   'лЪ

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2401) Then

      Tr = "лЪ"

   'Анунасика

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2305) Then

      Tr = "№"

   'Анусвара

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2306) Then

      Tr = "М"

   'оМ дебильный

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2384) Then

      Tr = "оМ"

   'Вирама

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2307) Then

     Tr = "Х"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2325) Then

     Tr = "к"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2326) Then

     Tr = "кх"

   'Аваграха

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2381) Then

     Tr = "@"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2327) Then

     Tr = "г"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2328) Then

     Tr = "гх"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2329) Then

     Tr = "Г"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2330) Then

      Tr = "ч"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2331) Then

      Tr = "чх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2332) Then

      Tr = "Ж"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2333) Then

      Tr = "Жх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2334) Then

      Tr = "Ь"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2335) Then

      Tr = "Т"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2336) Then

      Tr = "Тх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2337) Then

      Tr = "Д"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2338) Then

      Tr = "Дх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2339) Then

      Tr = "Н"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2340) Then

      Tr = "т"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2341) Then

      Tr = "тх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2342) Then

      Tr = "д"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2343) Then

      Tr = "дх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2344) Then

      Tr = "н"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2346) Then

      Tr = "п"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2347) Then

      Tr = "пх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2348) Then

      Tr = "б"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2349) Then

      Tr = "бх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2350) Then

      Tr = "м"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2351) Then

      Tr = "й"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2352) Then

      Tr = "р"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2355) Then

      Tr = "Л"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2354) Then

      Tr = "л"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2357) Then

      Tr = "в"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2358) Then

      Tr = "ш"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2359) Then

      Tr = "Ш"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2360) Then

      Tr = "с"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2361) Then

      Tr = "х"

   ' _ подчеркивание

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2386) Then

      Tr = "_"

  ' * надстрочный штрих

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2385) Then

   Tr = "*"

  ' '

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2365) Then

   Tr = "'"

  ' цифры

  ElseIf sim >= ChrW(2406) And sim <= ChrW(2415) Then

   Tr = Chr(AscW(sim) - 2358)

  Else

   Tr = sim

  End If

  End Function

  Потом закройте редактор VBA(Меню: File-Close)

  На переключатель на панели элементов в виде Угольника с карандашом, что-бы выйти из режима конструктора и программа будет работать.Рад что заработало.

  Объект не “надпись” должен быть, “поле”. Меню: вид-панели-элементы управления Выбираем поле, и раздвигаем его как в Pain прямоугольник на листе, затем два раза кликните по полю, вы попадете в модуль листа, это все удалите:

  Private Sub TextBox1_Change()

  End Sub

  А это вставьте.

  Private Sub TextBox1_Change()

    s = RusLat_San(TextBox1.Text)

   Cells(1, 1) = s

   TextBox1.Text = s

    Selection.AutoFilter Field:=1, Criteria1:="=" + s + "*", Operator:=xlAnd

  End Sub

  Private Sub TextBox1_KeyPress(ByVal KeyAscii As MSForms.ReturnInteger)

  'если R

  If SanRus(Mid(" " + TextBox1.Text, Len(TextBox1.Text) + 1)) = "Р" Then

   If InStr("РR", ChrW(KeyAscii)) <> 0 Then

     TextBox1.Text = RusLat_San(SanRus(TextBox1.Text) + ChrW(KeyAscii))

     KeyAscii = 0

   End If

  'если Вирама

  ElseIf Mid(" " + TextBox1.Text, Len(TextBox1.Text) + 1) = ChrW(2381) Then

   'если гласная

   If InStr("аАиИуУеЕоОРЪaAiIuUeEoORхh}", ChrW(KeyAscii)) <> 0 Then

     TextBox1.Text = RusLat_San(SanRus(TextBox1.Text) + ChrW(KeyAscii))

     KeyAscii = 0

   End If

  End If

  End Sub

  Дольше вставьте модуль макросов(Меню: вставка-модуль Insert-Module)

  И вставьте в модуль этот код:

  Function RusLat_San(srt)

  '

  ' RusLat_san Макрос

  ' Макрос записан 20.07.2013 Тихвинский В.И.

  ' Переделан в функцию 14.10.14

  '

   Dim i As Long

    s = "@" + srt + "  "

    dev = ""

   s1 = ""

   s = s + " "

   For i = 1 To Len(s) - 3

   'музыкальные знаки и анусвара или анунасика или висарга

    If (Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(784) Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(775) Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ":" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "Щ" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "M" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "М" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "№" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "#" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "Х" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "H" _

    Or AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 7745 Or AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 7747 Or AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 328 Or AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 7717) And (Mid(s, i, 1) = "*" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "_" Or AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 1648 Or AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 817) Then

     s1 = s1 + Mid(s, i + 1, 1) + Mid(s, i, 1)

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "n" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "g" Then

     s1 = s1 + "G"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "р" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "ы" Then

     s1 = s1 + "Р"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "л" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "ы" Then

     s1 = s1 + "лР"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "р" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "Ы" Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ъ"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "л" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "Ы" Then

     s1 = s1 + "лЪ"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "н" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "ь" Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ь"

     i = i + 1

    'Е -кириллица

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "Е" Then

     s1 = s1 + "е"

    'О -кириллица

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "О" Then

     s1 = s1 + "о"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "Й" Then

     s1 = s1 + "аи"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "В" Then

     s1 = s1 + "ау"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "К" Then

     s1 = s1 + "Г"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "З" Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ж"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "П" Then

     s1 = s1 + "Л"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "Ф" Then

     s1 = s1 + "х"

   'устранение КТ+lat придыхательные

    ElseIf InStr("кгчЖТДтдпб", Mid(s, i, 1)) <> 0 And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "h" Then

     s1 = s1 + Mid(s, i, 1) + "х"

     i = i + 1

    'устранение кодировки КТЮ

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "а" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "а" Then

     s1 = s1 + "А"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1110) And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(1110) Then

     s1 = s1 + "И"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1110) Then

     s1 = s1 + "и"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "у" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "у" Then

     s1 = s1 + "У"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "а" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(1111) And Not glKTU(Mid(s, i + 2, 1), Mid(s, i + 3, 1)) Then

     s1 = s1 + "аи"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "а" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "в" And Not glKTU(Mid(s, i + 2, 1), Mid(s, i + 3, 1)) Then

     s1 = s1 + "ау"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1167) And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(1167) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ъ"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1167) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Р"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1111) Then

     s1 = s1 + "й"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1108) Then

     s1 = s1 + "е"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1121) Then

     s1 = s1 + "о"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1189) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Г"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1115) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ж"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(1114) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ь"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(708) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Л"

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "т" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(61476) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Т"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "д" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(61476) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Д"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "н" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(61476) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Н"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "ш" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = ChrW(61476) Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ш"

     i = i + 1

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) = "Р" And (Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "Р" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "R") Then

     s1 = s1 + "Ъ"

     i = i + 1

      'запретные символы, мягкий знак и надстрочная дуга

    ElseIf Mid(s, i, 1) <> "ь" And Mid(s, i, 1) <> ChrW(865) Then

       s1 = s1 + Mid(s, i, 1)

    End If

   Next i

   s = s1 + "  "

   For i = 2 To Len(s) - 2

    sim0 = Mid(s, i - 1, 1)

   If sogl(sim0) And (Mid(s, i, 1) = "a" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "а") Then

    If Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "i" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "и" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "u" Or Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "у" Then

     sim = Mid(s, i, 1) + Mid(s, i + 1, 1) + "_"

     i = i + 1

    Else

     sim = ""

    End If

   ElseIf sogl(sim0) And (Mid(s, i, 1) = "А" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "A" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(257) Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "и" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "i" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "И" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "I" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(299) _

   Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "у" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "u" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "У" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "U" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(363) _

   Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "Р" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "R" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "Ъ" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "}" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(7771) Or Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(7773) _

   Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "о" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "o" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "е" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "e") Then

     sim = Mid(s, i, 1) + "_"

     If Mid(s, i, 1) = "R" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "R" Then

      sim = "}_"

      i = i + 1

     End If

   Else

    sim0 = Mid(s, i - 1, 1)

    sim = Mid(s, i, 1)

    If Mid(s, i, 1) = "R" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "R" Then

     sim = "}"

     i = i + 1

    End If

    sim1 = Mid(s, i + 1, 1)

   'придыхательные и ||

   If ((sim1 = "h" Or sim1 = "х") And (sim = "k" Or sim = "к" Or sim = "g" Or sim = "г" Or sim = "c" Or sim = "ч" _

   Or sim = "j" Or sim = "Ж" Or sim = "T" Or sim = "Т" Or sim = "D" Or sim = "Д" Or sim = "t" Or sim = "т" _

   Or sim = "d" Or sim = "д" Or sim = "p" Or sim = "п" Or sim = "b" Or sim = "б" Or sim = ChrW(7789) Or sim = ChrW(7693))) Or sim = "|" And sim1 = "|" Or sim = "n" And sim1 = "g" _

   Or sim = "a" And sim1 = "i" Or sim = "а" And sim1 = "и" Or sim = "a" And sim1 = "u" Or sim = "а" And sim1 = "у" Then

    sim = sim + sim1

    i = i + 1

   End If

   End If

   'служебные символы

    If sim = " " Then

    dev = dev + Chr(32)

    ElseIf sim = Chr(13) Then

    dev = dev + Chr(13)

    'табуляция

    ElseIf sim = Chr(10) Then

     dev = dev + Chr(10)

    'табуляция

    ElseIf sim = Chr(9) Then

     dev = dev + Chr(9)

     'Аваграха

    ElseIf sim = "'" Or sim = ChrW(8216) Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2365)

    ElseIf sim = "|" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2404)

    ElseIf sim = "||" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2405)

    'слоги

    'гласные и полугласные в роли гласных не в составе слога

    ElseIf sim = "а" Or sim = "a" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2309)

    ElseIf sim = "А" Or sim = "A" Or sim = ChrW(257) Then

        dev = dev + ChrW(2310)

    ElseIf sim = "и" Or sim = "i" Then

       dev = dev + ChrW(2311)

    ElseIf sim = "И" Or sim = "I" Or sim = ChrW(299) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2312)

    ElseIf sim = "у" Or sim = "u" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2313)

    ElseIf sim = "У" Or sim = "U" Or sim = ChrW(363) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2314)

    ElseIf sim = "Р" Or sim = "R" Or sim = ChrW(7771) Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2315)

    ElseIf sim = "Ъ" Or sim = "}" Or sim = ChrW(7773) Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2400)

    ElseIf sim = "А_" Or sim = "A_" Or sim = ChrW(257) + "_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2366)

    ElseIf sim = "и_" Or sim = "i_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2367)

    ElseIf sim = "И_" Or sim = "I_" Or sim = ChrW(299) + "_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2368)

    ElseIf sim = "у_" Or sim = "u_" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2369)

    ElseIf sim = "У_" Or sim = "U_" Or sim = ChrW(363) + "_" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2370)

    ElseIf sim = "Р_" Or sim = "R_" Or sim = ChrW(7771) + "_" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2371)

    ElseIf sim = "Ъ_" Or sim = "}_" Or sim = ChrW(7773) + "_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2372)

    'дифтонги

    ElseIf sim = "е" Or sim = "e" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2319)

    ElseIf sim = "аи" Or sim = "ai" Then

       dev = dev + ChrW(2320)

    ElseIf sim = "о" Or sim = "o" Then

       dev = dev + ChrW(2323)

    ElseIf sim = "ау" Or sim = "au" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2324)

    ElseIf sim = "е_" Or sim = "e_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2375)

    ElseIf sim = "аи_" Or sim = "ai_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2376)

    ElseIf sim = "о_" Or sim = "o_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2379)

    ElseIf sim = "ау_" Or sim = "au_" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2380)

    'Анунасика

    ElseIf sim = "№" Or sim = "#" Or sim = ChrW(328) Or sim = ChrW(784) Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2305)

    'Анусвара 7747 - c нижней точкой, 775 - верхняя точка

    ElseIf sim = "М" Or sim = "M" Or sim = ChrW(775) Or sim = ChrW(7745) Or sim = ChrW(7747) Or sim = "Щ" Then

        dev = dev + ChrW(2306)

    'Висарга

    ElseIf sim = "Х" Or sim = "H" Or sim = ChrW(7717) Or sim = "%" Or sim = ":" Then

        dev = dev + ChrW(2307)

    'согласные

    ElseIf sim = "к" Or sim = "k" Then

       dev = dev + ChrW(2325)

    ElseIf sim = "кх" Or sim = "kh" Then

    dev = dev + ChrW(2326)

    ElseIf sim = "г" Or sim = "g" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2327)

    ElseIf sim = "гх" Or sim = "gh" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2328)

    ElseIf sim = "Г" Or sim = "G" Or sim = ChrW(7749) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2329)

    ElseIf sim = "ч" Or sim = "c" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2330)

    ElseIf sim = "чх" Or sim = "ch" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2331)

    ElseIf sim = "Ж" Or sim = "j" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2332)

    ElseIf sim = "Жх" Or sim = "jh" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2333)

    ElseIf sim = "Ь" Or sim = "~" Or sim = "J" Or sim = ChrW(241) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2334)

    ElseIf sim = "Т" Or sim = "T" Or sim = ChrW(7789) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2335)

    ElseIf sim = "Тх" Or sim = "Th" Or sim = ChrW(7789) + "h" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2336)

    ElseIf sim = "Д" Or sim = "D" Or sim = ChrW(7693) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2337)

    ElseIf sim = "Дх" Or sim = "Dh" Or sim = ChrW(7693) + "h" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2338)

    ElseIf sim = "Н" Or sim = "N" Or sim = ChrW(7751) Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2339)

    ElseIf sim = "т" Or sim = "t" Then

     dev = dev + ChrW(2340)

    ElseIf sim = "тх" Or sim = "th" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2341)

    ElseIf sim = "д" Or sim = "d" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2342)

   ElseIf sim = "дх" Or sim = "dh" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2343)

   ElseIf sim = "н" Or sim = "n" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2344)

   ElseIf sim = "п" Or sim = "p" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2346)

   ElseIf sim = "пх" Or sim = "ph" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2347)

   ElseIf sim = "б" Or sim = "b" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2348)

   ElseIf sim = "бх" Or sim = "bh" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2349)

   ElseIf sim = "м" Or sim = "m" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2350)

   ElseIf sim = "й" Or sim = "y" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2351)

   ElseIf sim = "р" Or sim = "r" Then

        dev = dev + ChrW(2352)

   ElseIf sim = "Л" Or sim = "L" Or sim = ChrW(7735) Then

       dev = dev + ChrW(2355)

   ElseIf sim = "л" Or sim = "l" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2354)

   ElseIf sim = "в" Or sim = "v" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2357)

   ElseIf sim = "ш" Or sim = "C" Or sim = "z" Or sim = "Z" Or sim = ChrW(231) Or sim = ChrW(347) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2358)

   ElseIf sim = "Ш" Or sim = "S" Or sim = ChrW(7779) Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2359)

   ElseIf sim = "с" Or sim = "s" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2360)

   ElseIf sim = "х" Or sim = "h" Then

      dev = dev + ChrW(2361)

  ' цифры

  ElseIf sim >= "0" And sim <= "9" Then

   dev = dev + ChrW(Asc(sim) + 2358)

  ' _ подчеркивание

  ElseIf sim = "_" Or sim = ChrW(817) Then

   dev = dev + ChrW(2386)

  ' * надстрочный штрих

  ElseIf sim = "*" Or sim = ChrW(1648) Then

   dev = dev + ChrW(2385)

  ' '

  ElseIf sim = "'" Or sim = ChrW(8216) Then

   dev = dev + ChrW(2365)

  Else

   dev = dev + sim

  End If

  If Len(sim) = 2 Then

   'избавляемся от придыхания

   If Mid(sim, 2) = "х" Or Mid(sim, 2) = "h" Then

    sim = Mid(sim, 1, 1)

    sim1 = Mid(s, i + 1, 1)

   End If

  End If

  If sogl(sim) And (sogl(sim1) Or sim1 = " " Or sim1 = "|" Or sim1 = ChrW(13) Or sim1 = ChrW(10) Or sim1 = ChrW(9) Or sim1 = "," Or sim1 = ".") Then

    'вирама

    dev = dev + ChrW(2381)

  End If

  Next i

   RusLat_San = dev

  End Function

  'Дополнительные функции к перекодировщику

  Private Function glKTU(sim, SIM2)

  '

  ' sogl Макрос

  ' Макрос записан 20.07.2013 Тихвинский В.И.

  '

  glKTU = sim = "а" Or sim = ChrW(1110) Or sim = "у" Or sim = ChrW(1108) Or sim = ChrW(1121) _

   Or sim = ChrW(1167) Or sim = "л" And SIM2 = ChrW(1167)

  End Function

  Private Function sogl(sim)

  '

  ' sogl Макрос

  ' Макрос записан 20.07.2013 Тихвинский В.И.

  '

  sogl = (sim = "k" Or sim = "к" Or sim = "g" Or sim = "г" Or sim = "c" Or sim = "ч" Or sim = "н" Or sim = "n" _

   Or sim = "j" Or sim = "Ж" Or sim = "T" Or sim = "Т" Or sim = "D" Or sim = "Д" Or sim = "t" Or sim = "т" _

   Or sim = "d" Or sim = "д" Or sim = "p" Or sim = "п" Or sim = "b" Or sim = "б" Or sim = ChrW(7789) Or sim = ChrW(7693) _

   Or sim = "G" Or sim = "Г" Or sim = ChrW(7749) Or sim = "Ь" Or sim = "~" Or sim = ChrW(241) Or sim = "N" Or sim = "Н" Or sim = ChrW(7751) _

   Or sim = "m" Or sim = "м" Or sim = "y" Or sim = "й" Or sim = "r" Or sim = "р" Or sim = "l" Or sim = "л" Or sim = "L" Or sim = "Л" Or sim = ChrW(7735) _

   Or sim = "v" Or sim = "в" Or sim = "C" Or sim = "ш" Or sim = ChrW(231) Or sim = "S" Or sim = "Ш" Or sim = ChrW(7779) Or sim = "s" Or sim = "с" Or sim = "h" Or sim = "х" _

   Or sim = "J" Or sim = "z" Or sim = "Z") ' J,z,Z дополнительная кодировка для Гарвард-Киото(Harvard-Kyoto)

  End Function

  Public Function SanRus(s)

  '

  ' SanRus Макрос

  ' Макрос записан 4.08.2013 Тихвинский В.И.

  ' Переделан в функцию 17.10.14

  '

  Dim s1 As String, i As Long

  s = s + " "

  s1 = ""

  For i = 1 To Len(s) - 1

   If (AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 2385 Or AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 2386) And (AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 2305 Or AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 2306 Or AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 2307) Then

    s1 = s1 + Mid(s, i + 1, 1) + Mid(s, i, 1)

    i = i + 1

   Else

    s1 = s1 + Mid(s, i, 1)

   End If

  Next i

  s = s1

  s1 = ""

  For i = 1 To Len(s)

    s1 = s1 + Tr(Mid(s, i, 1))

  Next i

  s1 = s1 + "@"

  s = ""

  For i = 1 To Len(s1) - 1

  'с придыхательными

  If InStr("кгчЖТДтдпб", Mid(s1, i, 1)) <> 0 And Mid(s1, i + 1, 1) = "х" Then

   s = s + Mid(s1, i, 1)

  'гласная а

  ElseIf InStr("кгГчЖЬТДНтднпбмйрЛлвшШсх", Mid(s1, i, 1)) <> 0 And InStr("аАиИуУРЪео@", Mid(s1, i + 1, 1)) = 0 Then

   s = s + Mid(s1, i, 1) + "а"

  ElseIf Mid(s1, i, 1) <> "@" Then

   s = s + Mid(s1, i, 1)

  End If

  Next i

   SanRus = s

  End Function

  Private Function Tr(sim)

  '

  ' Tr Макрос

  ' Макрос записан 03.03.2012 Тихвинский В.И.

  '

    'служебные символы

    If sim = " " Then

    Tr = Chr(32)

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(13) Then

    Tr = Chr(13)

    'аваграха

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2365) Then

     Tr = "'"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2404) Then

    Tr = "|"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2405) Then

    Tr = "||"

    'слоги

    'гласные и полугласные в роли гласных

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2309) Then

      Tr = "а"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2310) Or sim = ChrW(2366) Then

      Tr = "А"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2311) Or sim = ChrW(2367) Then

     Tr = "и"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2312) Or sim = ChrW(2368) Then

     Tr = "И"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2313) Or sim = ChrW(2369) Then

     Tr = "у"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2314) Or sim = ChrW(2370) Then

     Tr = "У"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2315) Or sim = ChrW(2371) Then

     Tr = "Р"

    ElseIf sim = ChrW(2400) Or sim = ChrW(2372) Then

     Tr = "Ъ"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2319) Or sim = ChrW(2375) Then

      Tr = "е"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2320) Or sim = ChrW(2376) Then

      Tr = "аи"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2323) Or sim = ChrW(2379) Then

      Tr = "о"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2324) Or sim = ChrW(2380) Then

      Tr = "ау"

   'лР

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2402) Then

      Tr = "@лР"

   'лР

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2316) Then

      Tr = "лР"

   'лЪ

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2403) Then

      Tr = "@лЪ"

   'лЪ

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2401) Then

      Tr = "лЪ"

   'Анунасика

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2305) Then

      Tr = "№"

   'Анусвара

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2306) Then

      Tr = "М"

   'оМ дебильный

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2384) Then

      Tr = "оМ"

   'Вирама

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2307) Then

     Tr = "Х"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2325) Then

     Tr = "к"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2326) Then

     Tr = "кх"

   'Аваграха

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2381) Then

     Tr = "@"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2327) Then

     Tr = "г"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2328) Then

     Tr = "гх"

   ElseIf sim = ChrW(2329) Then

     Tr = "Г"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2330) Then

      Tr = "ч"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2331) Then

      Tr = "чх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2332) Then

      Tr = "Ж"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2333) Then

      Tr = "Жх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2334) Then

      Tr = "Ь"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2335) Then

      Tr = "Т"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2336) Then

      Tr = "Тх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2337) Then

      Tr = "Д"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2338) Then

      Tr = "Дх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2339) Then

      Tr = "Н"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2340) Then

      Tr = "т"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2341) Then

      Tr = "тх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2342) Then

      Tr = "д"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2343) Then

      Tr = "дх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2344) Then

      Tr = "н"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2346) Then

      Tr = "п"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2347) Then

      Tr = "пх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2348) Then

      Tr = "б"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2349) Then

      Tr = "бх"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2350) Then

      Tr = "м"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2351) Then

      Tr = "й"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2352) Then

      Tr = "р"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2355) Then

      Tr = "Л"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2354) Then

      Tr = "л"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2357) Then

      Tr = "в"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2358) Then

      Tr = "ш"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2359) Then

      Tr = "Ш"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2360) Then

      Tr = "с"

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2361) Then

      Tr = "х"

   ' _ подчеркивание

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2386) Then

      Tr = "_"

  ' * надстрочный штрих

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2385) Then

   Tr = "*"

  ' '

  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2365) Then

   Tr = "'"

  ' цифры

  ElseIf sim >= ChrW(2406) And sim <= ChrW(2415) Then

   Tr = Chr(AscW(sim) - 2358)

  Else

   Tr = sim

  End If

  End Function

  Потом закройте редактор VBA(Меню: File-Close)

  На переключатель на панели элементов в виде Угольника с карандашом, что-бы выйти из режима конструктора и программа будет работать.

> /   i = i + 1/

>

> /  Else/

>

> /   sim = ""/

>

> /  End If/

>

> //

>

> / ElseIf sogl(sim0) And (Mid(s, i, 1) = "А" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "A" Or 

> Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(257) Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "и" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "i" 

> Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "И" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "I" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(299) _/

>

> / Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "у" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "u" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "У" Or 

> Mid(s, i, 1) = "U" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(363) _/

>

> / Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "Р" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "R" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "Ъ" Or 

> Mid(s, i, 1) = "}" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = ChrW(7771) Or Mid(s, i, 1) = 

> ChrW(7773) _/

>

> / Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "о" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "o" Or Mid(s, i, 1) = "е" Or 

> Mid(s, i, 1) = "e") Then/

>

> /   sim = Mid(s, i, 1) + "_"/

>

> //

>

> /   If Mid(s, i, 1) = "R" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "R" Then/

>

> /    sim = "}_"/

>

> /    i = i + 1/

>

> /   End If/

>

> //

>

> / Else/

>

> /  sim0 = Mid(s, i - 1, 1)/

>

> /  sim = Mid(s, i, 1)/

>

> //

>

> /  If Mid(s, i, 1) = "R" And Mid(s, i + 1, 1) = "R" Then/

>

> /   sim = "}"/

>

> /   i = i + 1/

>

> /  End If/

>

> //

>

> /  sim1 = Mid(s, i + 1, 1)/

>

> //

>

> / 'придыхательные и ||/

>

> / If ((sim1 = "h" Or sim1 = "х") And (sim = "k" Or sim = "к" Or sim = 

> "g" Or sim = "г" Or sim = "c" Or sim = "ч" _/

>

> / Or sim = "j" Or sim = "Ж" Or sim = "T" Or sim = "Т" Or sim = "D" Or 

> sim = "Д" Or sim = "t" Or sim = "т" _/

>

> / Or sim = "d" Or sim = "д" Or sim = "p" Or sim = "п" Or sim = "b" Or 

> sim = "б" Or sim = ChrW(7789) Or sim = ChrW(7693))) Or sim = "|" And 

> sim1 = "|" Or sim = "n" And sim1 = "g" _/

>

> / Or sim = "a" And sim1 = "i" Or sim = "а" And sim1 = "и" Or sim = "a" 

> And sim1 = "u" Or sim = "а" And sim1 = "у" Then/

>

> ///sim = sim + sim1/

>

> /  i = i + 1/

>

> ///End If/

>

> //

>

> / End If/

>

> //

>

> / 'служебные символы/

>

> /  If sim = " " Then/

>

> /  dev = dev + Chr(32)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = Chr(13) Then/

>

> /  dev = dev + Chr(13)/

>

> /  'табуляция/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = Chr(10) Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + Chr(10)/

>

> /  'табуляция/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = Chr(9) Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + Chr(9)/

> /   'Аваграха/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "'" Or sim = ChrW(8216) Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + ChrW(2365)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "|" Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + ChrW(2404)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "||" Then/

>

> /   dev//= //dev//+ //ChrW//(2405)/

>

> /  'слоги/

> /  'гласные и полугласные в роли гласных не в составе слога/

> ///ElseIf sim = "а" Or sim = "a" Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + ChrW(2309)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "А" Or sim = "A" Or sim = ChrW(257) Then/

>

> /      dev = dev + ChrW(2310)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "и" Or sim = "i" Then/

>

> /     dev = dev + ChrW(2311)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "И" Or sim = "I" Or sim = ChrW(299) Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2312)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "у" Or sim = "u" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2313)/

>

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2314)/

>

>

> /   dev = dev + ChrW(2315)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "Ъ" Or sim = "}" Or sim = ChrW(7773) Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + ChrW(2400)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "А_" Or sim = "A_" Or sim = ChrW(257) + "_" Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + ChrW(2366)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "и_" Or sim = "i_" Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + ChrW(2367)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "И_" Or sim = "I_" Or sim = ChrW(299) + "_" Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + ChrW(2368)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "у_" Or sim = "u_" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2369)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "У_" Or sim = "U_" Or sim = ChrW(363) + "_" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2370)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "Р_" Or sim = "R_" Or sim = ChrW(7771) + "_" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2371)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "Ъ_" Or sim = "}_" Or sim = ChrW(7773) + "_" Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + ChrW(2372)/

>

> /  'дифтонги/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "е" Or sim = "e" Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + ChrW(2319)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "аи" Or sim = "ai" Then/

>

> /     dev = dev + ChrW(2320)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "о" Or sim = "o" Then/

>

> /     dev = dev + ChrW(2323)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "ау" Or sim = "au" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2324)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "е_" Or sim = "e_" Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + ChrW(2375)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "аи_" Or sim = "ai_" Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + ChrW(2376)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "о_" Or sim = "o_" Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + ChrW(2379)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "ау_" Or sim = "au_" Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + ChrW(2380)/

>

> /  'Анунасика/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "№" Or sim = "#" Or sim = ChrW(328) Or sim = ChrW(784) 

> Then/

>

> /   dev//= //dev//+ //ChrW//(2305)/

>

> /  'Анусвара 7747 - //c//нижней точкой, 775 - верхняя точка/

>

> ///ElseIf sim = "М" Or sim = "M" Or sim = ChrW(775) Or sim = 

> ChrW(7745) Or sim = ChrW(7747) Or sim = "Щ" Then/

>

> /      dev = dev + ChrW(2306)/

>

> /  'Висарга/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "Х" Or sim = "H" Or sim = ChrW(7717) Or sim = "%" Or 

> sim = ":" Then/

>

> /      dev = dev + ChrW(2307)/

>

> /  'согласные/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "к" Or sim = "k" Then/

>

> /     dev = dev + ChrW(2325)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "кх" Or sim = "kh" Then/

>

> /  dev = dev + ChrW(2326)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "г" Or sim = "g" Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + ChrW(2327)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "гх" Or sim = "gh" Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + ChrW(2328)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "Г" Or sim = "G" Or sim = ChrW(7749) Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2329)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "ч" Or sim = "c" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2330)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "чх" Or sim = "ch" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2331)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "Ж" Or sim = "j" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2332)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "Жх" Or sim = "jh" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2333)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "Ь" Or sim = "~" Or sim = "J" Or sim = ChrW(241) Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2334)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "Т" Or sim = "T" Or sim = ChrW(7789) Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2335)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "Тх" Or sim = "Th" Or sim = ChrW(7789) + "h" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2336)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "Д" Or sim = "D" Or sim = ChrW(7693) Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2337)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "Дх" Or sim = "Dh" Or sim = ChrW(7693) + "h" Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + ChrW(2338)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "Н" Or sim = "N" Or sim = ChrW(7751) Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + ChrW(2339)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "т" Or sim = "t" Then/

>

> /   dev = dev + ChrW(2340)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "тх" Or sim = "th" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2341)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = "д" Or sim = "d" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2342)/

>

> / ElseIf sim = "дх" Or sim = "dh" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2343)/

>

> / ElseIf sim = "н" Or sim = "n" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2344)/

>

> / ElseIf sim = "п" Or sim = "p" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2346)/

>

> / ElseIf sim = "пх" Or sim = "ph" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2347)/

>

> / ElseIf sim = "б" Or sim = "b" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2348)/

>

> / ElseIf sim = "бх" Or sim = "bh" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2349)/

>

> / ElseIf sim = "м" Or sim = "m" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2350)/

>

> / ElseIf sim = "й" Or sim = "y" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2351)/

>

> / ElseIf sim = "р" Or sim = "r" Then/

>

> /      dev = dev + ChrW(2352)/

>

> / ElseIf sim = "Л" Or sim = "L" Or sim = ChrW(7735) Then/

>

> /     dev = dev + ChrW(2355)/

>

> / ElseIf sim = "л" Or sim = "l" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2354)/

>

> / ElseIf sim = "в" Or sim = "v" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2357)/

>

> / ElseIf sim = "ш" Or sim = "C" Or sim = "z" Or sim = "Z" Or sim = 

> ChrW(231) Or sim = ChrW(347) Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2358)/

>

> / ElseIf sim = "Ш" Or sim = "S" Or sim = ChrW(7779) Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2359)/

>

> / ElseIf sim = "с" Or sim = "s" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2360)/

>

> / ElseIf sim = "х" Or sim = "h" Then/

>

> /    dev = dev + ChrW(2361)/

>

> /' цифры/

>

> /ElseIf sim >= "0" And sim <= "9" Then/

>

> / dev = dev + ChrW(Asc(sim) + 2358)/

>

> /' _ подчеркивание/

>

> /ElseIf sim = "_" Or sim = ChrW(817) Then/

>

> / dev = dev + ChrW(2386)/

>

> /' * надстрочный штрих/

>

> /ElseIf sim = "*" Or sim = ChrW(1648) Then/

>

> / dev = dev + ChrW(2385)/

>

> /' '/

>

> /ElseIf sim = "'" Or sim = ChrW(8216) Then/

>

> / dev = dev + ChrW(2365)/

>

> /Else/

>

> / dev = dev + sim/

>

> /End If/

>

> //

>

> /If Len(sim) = 2 Then/

>

> / 'избавляемся от придыхания/

>

> / If Mid(sim, 2) = "х" Or Mid(sim, 2) = "h" Then/

>

> /  sim = Mid(sim, 1, 1)/

>

> /  sim1 = Mid(s, i + 1, 1)/

>

> / End If/

>

> /End If/

>

> //

>

> /If sogl(sim) And (sogl(sim1) Or sim1 = " " Or sim1 = "|" Or sim1 = 

> ChrW(13) Or sim1 = ChrW(10) Or sim1 = ChrW(9) Or sim1 = "," Or sim1 = 

> ".") Then/

>

> /  'вирама/

>

> /  dev = dev + ChrW(2381)/

>

> /End If/

>

> //

>

> /Next i/

>

> //

>

> / RusLat_San = dev/

>

> /End////Function///

> /Private////Function////glKTU//(//sim//, //SIM//2)/

>

> /'/

>

> /' //sogl//Макрос/

>

> /' Макрос записан 20.07.2013 Тихвинский В.И./

>

> /'/

>

> /glKTU = sim = "а" Or sim = ChrW(1110) Or sim = "у" Or sim = 

> ChrW(1108) Or sim = ChrW(1121) _/

>

> / Or sim = ChrW(1167) Or sim = "л" And SIM2 = ChrW(1167)/

>

> //

>

> /End Function/

>

> /Private Function sogl(sim)/

>

> /'/

>

> /' //sogl//Макрос/

>

> /' Макрос записан 20.07.2013 Тихвинский В.И./

>

> /'/

>

> /sogl = (sim = "k" Or sim = "к" Or sim = "g" Or sim = "г" Or sim = "c" 

> Or sim = "ч" Or sim = "н" Or sim = "n" _/

>

> / Or sim = "j" Or sim = "Ж" Or sim = "T" Or sim = "Т" Or sim = "D" Or 

> sim = "Д" Or sim = "t" Or sim = "т" _/

>

> / Or sim = "d" Or sim = "д" Or sim = "p" Or sim = "п" Or sim = "b" Or 

> sim = "б" Or sim = ChrW(7789) Or sim = ChrW(7693) _/

>

> / Or sim = "G" Or sim = "Г" Or sim = ChrW(7749) Or sim = "Ь" Or sim = 

> "~" Or sim = ChrW(241) Or sim = "N" Or sim = "Н" Or sim = ChrW(7751) _/

>

> / Or sim = "m" Or sim = "м" Or sim = "y" Or sim = "й" Or sim = "r" Or 

> sim = "р" Or sim = "l" Or sim = "л" Or sim = "L" Or sim = "Л" Or sim = 

> ChrW(7735) _/

>

> / Or sim = "v" Or sim = "в" Or sim = "C" Or sim = "ш" Or sim = 

> ChrW(231) Or sim = "S" Or sim = "Ш" Or sim = ChrW(7779) Or sim = "s" 

> Or sim = "с" Or sim = "h" Or sim = "х" _/

>

> / Or sim = "J" Or sim = "z" Or sim = "Z") ' J,z,Z дополнительная 

> кодировка для Гарвард-Киото(Harvard-Kyoto)/

>

> //

>

> /End Function/

>

>

> //

>

> /Public Function SanRus(s)/

>

> /'/

>

> /' //SanRus//Макрос/

>

> /' Макрос записан 4.08.2013 Тихвинский В.И./

>

> /' Переделан в функцию 17.10.14/

>

> /'/

>

> //

>

> /Dim s1 As String, i As Long/

>

> //

>

> //

>

> /s = s + " "/

>

> /s1 = ""/

>

> //

>

> /For i = 1 To Len(s) - 1/

>

> / If (AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 2385 Or AscW(Mid(s, i + 1, 1)) = 2386) 

> And (AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 2305 Or AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 2306 Or 

> AscW(Mid(s, i, 1)) = 2307) Then/

>

> /  s1 = s1 + Mid(s, i + 1, 1) + Mid(s, i, 1)/

>

> /  i = i + 1/

>

> / Else/

>

> /  s1 = s1 + Mid(s, i, 1)/

>

> / End If/

>

> /Next i/

>

> //

>

> //

>

> /s = s1/

>

> //

>

> /s1 = ""/

>

> /For i = 1 To Len(s)/

>

> /  s1 = s1 + Tr(Mid(s, i, 1))/

>

> /Next i/

>

> //

>

> /s1 = s1 + "@"/

>

> //

>

> /s = ""/

>

> //

>

> /For i = 1 To Len(s1) - 1/

>

> //

>

> /'с придыхательными/

>

> /If InStr("кгчЖТДтдпб", Mid(s1, i, 1)) <> 0 And Mid(s1, i + 1, 1) = 

> "х" Then/

>

> / s = s + Mid(s1, i, 1)/

>

> /'гласная а/

>

> /ElseIf InStr("кгГчЖЬТДНтднпбмйрЛлвшШсх", Mid(s1, i, 1)) <> 0 And 

> InStr("аАиИуУРЪео@", Mid(s1, i + 1, 1)) = 0 Then/

>

> / s = s + Mid(s1, i, 1) + "а"/

>

> /ElseIf Mid(s1, i, 1) <> "@" Then/

>

> / s = s + Mid(s1, i, 1)/

>

> /End If/

>

> //

>

> /Next i/

>

> //

>

> / SanRus = s/

>

> /End Function/

>

> //

>

> /Private Function Tr(sim)/

>

> /'/

>

> /' Tr Макрос/

>

> /' Макрос записан 03.03.2012 Тихвинский В.И./

>

> /'/

>

> /  'служебные символы/

>

> ///If////sim//= " " //Then///

>

> ///Tr = Chr(32)/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = ChrW(13) Then/

>

> /  Tr = Chr(13)/

>

>

> /  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2365) Then/

>

> /   Tr = "'"/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2404) Then/

> /  Tr = "|"/

>

> /  ElseIf sim = ChrW(2405) Then/

>

> /  Tr = "||"/

>

> //

> ///'слоги/

> /  'гласные и полугласные в роли гласных/

>

> ///ElseIf sim = ChrW(2309) Then/
